LoanRow (54-67) carries neither derived field.

### Every FR-LIB-002 action — issue, return, settle/waive fine — is unreachable in the UI, so the loans tab is a read-only queue and "Only unpaid fines" is a worklist with no way to work it. — `(school)/school/library/page.tsx`

- **Status:** Closed — `Issue a book` posts `POST /library/transactions` with the borrower picker following `borrower_type` (the schema forbids the other two ids); `Return` and `Fine` patch the two routes per row, offered only where the loan is open or something is owed. Verified live: a back-dated loan returned 25 days late computed a 62.50 fine, and a 20.00 part-payment left `42.50 outstanding · 20.00 of 62.50 paid`.
- **Category:** `ux` · line 250
- **What a user sees:** A librarian holding `library.issue` cannot issue a book, record a return, or mark a fine paid or waived anywhere in the product — the three verbs SRS §20.4 names.
- **Evidence:** library/page.tsx:250-257 is the `Only unpaid fines` checkbox and the panel's last control; LoansPanel has no PageHeader action and its Column[] (:188-230) contains no button, while BooksPanel gates `<a href="/school/library/books/new">Add book</a>` on `can('library.manage')` (:142-149). The endpoints exist: library.routes.js `router.post('/transactions', requirePermission('library.issue'), …)`, `router.patch('/transactions/:id/return', …)`, `router.patch('/transactions/:id/fine', …)` (122-144).
- **Fix:** Add the three controls to LoansPanel gated on `can('library.issue')`: a header action posting to `/library/transactions`, and per-row "Record return" / "Settle fine" in a Modal or ConfirmDialog from components/overlay.tsx patching the two routes, then `reload()`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** A grep of frontend/src for `library/transactions|settleFine|fine_waived|fine_paid|/return'` returns only library/page.tsx:7 (a comment), :63, :64, :186 (the useCollection read), :219 and :220 — no post or patch anywhere. The routes are real; the cited range is 122-144 rather than 120-145, which is the same block off by two. No SRS conflict: the fix reuses the existing `library.issue` permission and existing routes.

### The Borrower column shows only the borrower type, never who the borrower is. — `(school)/school/library/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `ux` · line 199
- **What a user sees:** Twenty rows that all read "student". A librarian chasing an overdue copy cannot tell which student holds it.
- **Evidence:** library/page.tsx:199 `{ key: 'borrower', header: 'Borrower', cell: (row) => row.borrower_type.replace(/_/g, ' ') },`. constants.js:553-557 `const LIBRARY_BORROWER_TYPES = Object.freeze({ STUDENT: 'student', TEACHER: 'teacher', STAFF: 'staff', });` — three possible words. The endpoint cannot name the borrower: library.service.js:373-381 includes only `{ model: db.Book, as: 'book', attributes: ['id', 'title', 'author', 'isbn', 'fine_per_day'] }`, and `student_id`/`teacher_id`/`staff_id` are not on LoanRow (:54-67). The screen's header (:285-288) promises "The catalogue, and who currently holds what."
- **Fix:** Backend: add the borrower to listTransactions's include (the mapping already exists as BORROWER_COLUMN/BORROWER_MODEL, library.service.js:77-88). Frontend: render the name with the type as secondary text; until then declare the borrower ids and print one the way the book cell falls back to `book #{row.book_id}`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Line 199 is verbatim, the constants block is exactly at 553-557, and the include at 373-381 is exactly the single Book join quoted. The panel's own argument for the book column is real at :193-197 ("a librarian does not know book 412 by its number"), which is the same standard. The fix adds no table, column or permission, so it does not touch the fixed SRS catalogue.

### The Results screen is a publication queue whose only control is a publication filter, but nothing in the entire frontend can publish a result, generate one, or open a result card. — `(school)/school/results/page.tsx`

- **Status:** Closed — `Calculate` and `Publish` on every exam row post `POST /exams/:id/results` and `POST /exams/:id/publish` behind their own confirmations, and every result row has a card download. Verified live: publishing before generating was refused 409 and the dialog stayed open carrying *"Results must be generated before they can be published"*.
- **Category:** `ux` · line 344
- **What a user sees:** A principal filters to "Not published", sees finished results, and there is no control anywhere in the product that publishes them or opens the result card. The empty state instructs the user to perform a step the product cannot perform.
- **Evidence:** results/page.tsx:344-347 `<PageHeader title="Results" description="Calculated results across every exam, ordered by class position..." />`; the header's justification for the filter is at :41-43 and the empty state at :400 says results "appear once an exam's marks are submitted and its results are generated." Backend: exams.routes.js:165-170 `'/results/:id'` → controller.showResult, :227-232 GET `'/:id/results'`, :234-240 POST `'/:id/results'` with `requirePermission('results.generate')` (:236), :242-248 POST `'/:id/publish'` with `requirePermission('results.generate')` (:244).
- **Fix:** Add a per-row link to the result card (`GET /exams/results/:id`, via `download()` at lib/apiClient.ts:461) and a publish action on the exam screen wired to `POST /exams/:id/publish`, gated on `can('results.generate')`, behind a ConfirmDialog.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Every quotation checks out. `grep -rn "api.post(" app/` returns exactly 22 call sites and none targets `/exams/:id/results`, `/exams/:id/publish` or a result card; `grep -rn "api.patch\|api.del"` over frontend/src returns nothing at all. exams/page.tsx's only `href=` is line 322 `href="/school/exams/new"`. The routes exist verbatim at exams.routes.js:166, :228/:229, :235/:236, :243/:244. The one imprecision is cosmetic: showResult is at exams.controller.js:178 and only streams PDF under `if (req.query.format === REPORT_FORMATS.PDF)`. The gap is reachable by a real user on a real screen, and the proposed fix invents no table, permission or route — `results.generate` is already in the catalogue.

### Rows carry no exam identity and there is no exam filter, so two results for the same student from two different exams render as visually identical duplicate rows. — `(school)/school/results/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `ux` · line 57
- **What a user sees:** In a school with more than one exam per term the table repeats the same child with different marks and no way to tell which exam each row is; `position ASC` interleaves them, so adjacent rows for one student are unrelated exams.
- **Evidence:** results/page.tsx:56-62 states it: "`listResults` includes exactly one association — `student`, with five attributes. It does **not** join `Exam`, `Class` or `Section`... with no exam column, two rows for the same student from two different exams are indistinguishable here." exams.service.js:1326-1328 `include: [{ model: db.Student, as: 'student', attributes: ['id', 'student_id', 'roll_number', 'first_name', 'last_name'] }]` and :1329 `order: getSort({ query }, RESULT_SORTABLE, ['position', 'ASC'])`. The five accepted filters are exams.validation.js:313-322; the screen wires only `is_published` (:203).
- **Fix:** Add an `Exam` include to `exams.service.js` `listResults`, render an Exam column, and add an exam picker fed from `GET /exams`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The header comment is verbatim at :56-62 and the service is exactly as described: I read exams.service.js:1312-1333 and the include list contains only `db.Student`; the filter loop at :1318 is `for (const field of ['exam_id', 'student_id', 'class_id', 'section_id', 'is_published'])`. The query built at results/page.tsx:202-205 passes only `page`, `limit` and `is_published`, so the endpoint is genuinely cross-exam. The fix touches a service include and a column — no new table, column or permission.

### The screen documents deactivation as the entire staff lifecycle, but there is no deactivate control on any row and the frontend issues no PATCH request anywhere. — `(school)/school/staff/page.tsx`

- **Status:** Closed — a per-row Deactivate/Reactivate control patching `PATCH /staff/:id`. It records `left_at` as well as the flag, because the update schema accepts both and a departure has a date; reactivation clears it back to null.
- **Category:** `ux` · line 33
- **What a user sees:** When someone leaves, the school cannot deactivate them from anywhere in the product. Because `staff_limit` counts `is_active: true`, the seat stays consumed and the school hits `PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` on the next hire with no way to free an allowance.
- **Evidence:** staff/page.tsx:42-44: "That flag is also the school's headcount: `staff_limit` counts `is_active: true` (`usageService.HEADCOUNT_SOURCES`), which is why deactivating rather than deleting is the whole lifecycle here — §15.4 names no deletion and the router mounts no DELETE." staff.routes.js:89-95 mounts `router.patch('/:id', requirePermission('staff.manage'), validate({ params: schemas.idParam, body: schemas.update }), ...)` and staff.validation.js:114 puts `is_active: fields.is_active` in that update schema. The columns array ends at the Status badge (staff/page.tsx:225-234) with no action column.
- **Fix:** Add a row action (or a staff detail route) that issues `PATCH /staff/:id` with `{ is_active: false }`, behind a ConfirmDialog and gated on `can('staff.manage')`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The header quote is verbatim at :42-44. staff.routes.js:89-95 is exactly that PATCH; staff.validation.js:114 is exactly `is_active: fields.is_active` inside `const update = Joi.object({` at :108. usageService.js:115-119 confirms the consequence: `[LIMITS.STAFF_LIMIT]: { model: 'Staff', where: { is_active: true }, label: 'active staff' }`. `find app -name page.tsx` lists only staff/page.tsx and staff/new/page.tsx, and no `api.patch` exists anywhere in frontend/src — so the route is genuinely unreachable and the seat is genuinely unrecoverable.

### The salary input is `type="number"`, so a value the browser cannot parse is read back as the empty string and the field is silently dropped from the body — the exact failure the comment above the submit claims to avoid. — `(school)/school/staff/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `money` · line 508
- **What a user sees:** An administrator enters "42,000" (or pastes "₹42,000"), submits, and the staff record is created with no salary. No error, no field message, and no edit screen anywhere in the product to correct it.
- **Evidence:** staff/new/page.tsx:505-516 `<Field id="salary" label="Salary" type="number" min={0} max={999999999999.99} step="0.01" value={values.salary} onChange={set('salary')} ... />`. The comment at :274-280 claims "`salary` goes as the string that was typed rather than through `Number()`... so \"42000\" arrives as a number and \"42,000\" is answered by the server's own reading of it." But :282-286 is `for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(values)) { if (key === 'metadata') continue; const trimmed = value.trim(); if (trimmed) body[key] = trimmed; }` — and an `<input type="number">` holding an unparseable value reports `value === ''`, so the figure never reaches `body`. staff.validation.js:60 `salary: Joi.number().min(0).max(999999999999.99).allow(null)` is optional in `create` (:91-100), so the record saves with salary null.
- **Fix:** Make salary `type="text"` with `inputMode="decimal"` so the typed characters survive to the body and Joi answers a bad figure with a 422 bound to the field; correct or delete the comment at :274-280.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Both quotations are verbatim (:505-516 and :274-286). components/form.tsx:93-113 spreads `{...input}` onto a bare `<input>`, so `type="number"` reaches the DOM, and `set('salary')` stores `event.target.value` — which the HTML value-sanitization algorithm makes `''` for any unparseable content (Firefox leaves the typed text visible while reporting `''`). staff.validation.js:91-100 `const create` requires only employee_id, category, first_name and joining_date, so the POST succeeds and the figure is gone. models/people.js:259 `salary: money({ allowNull: true, defaultValue: null })` and models/columns.js:60-61 `DataTypes.DECIMAL(14, 2)` are both exact. The comment's claim that the server answers "42,000" is provably false because the string never leaves the client.

### When the account list fails to load the screen tells the user to add the link later "from the Staff record itself", and no staff record screen exists; the `<label htmlFor="user_id">` above it is also left pointing at an element that is not rendered. — `(school)/school/staff/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `dead-link` · line 591
- **What a user sees:** A user who hits a `users.view` refusal is told to finish the job on a screen that does not exist, so the staff member is created permanently unlinked to a login. A screen-reader user also gets a "Linked account" label bound to nothing.
- **Evidence:** staff/new/page.tsx:587-595: `<label htmlFor="user_id" className="block text-sm font-medium">Linked account</label>` then `{usersFailed ? (<p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">The account list could not be loaded, so a login cannot be linked here. The staff record can still be created, and the link added later from the Staff record itself.</p>) : (` — the `<select id="user_id">` at :611-625 lives only in the non-failed branch. No `staff/[id]` route exists and no `api.patch` call exists anywhere in frontend/src, so `PATCH /staff/:id` (staff.routes.js:89) is unreachable.
- **Fix:** Move the label inside the non-failed branch, and change the sentence to say the link cannot be made until the account list is available — do not name a Staff record screen until one exists.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Verbatim at :587-595 and :611-625; the only `id="user_id"` in the file is at :612, inside the branch skipped when `usersFailed`. The refusal is routine rather than exotic: config/permissions.js:345-359 gives ROLES.RECEPTIONIST `'students.manage'`, `'parents.manage'` and so on but not `users.view`, and users.routes.js:66-68 gates `GET /users` on `requirePermission('users.view')` — so the exact user who admits people hits this branch. The named destination genuinely does not exist.

### On a 28-field form, a 422 that produces only per-field errors explicitly nulls the top-level banner, so the submit button the user is looking at reports nothing at all. — `(school)/school/students/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `missing-state` · line 453
- **What a user sees:** The user clicks "Admit student", the button un-busies, and the visible page is unchanged; the only sign of failure is a red line beside a field several screens up. It reads as a dead button.
- **Evidence:** students/new/page.tsx:453-460 `setFieldErrors(perField); setError(homeless.length ? homeless.join(' ') : Object.keys(perField).length ? null : caught.message);` — with every detail bound to a control, `error` is `null` and `{error ? <Notice tone="error">{error}</Notice> : null}` at :504 renders nothing. `EMPTY_VALUES` (:220-249) has 28 keys, the submit button is at :1014-1017 and `first_name` at :507. No `scrollIntoView`, `focus()` or `useRef` appears in the file.
- **Fix:** After `setFieldErrors(perField)`, focus or scroll to the first control named in `perField`, or keep a summary `Notice` listing the failed fields.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The setError chain is verbatim at :453-460 (formatted over multiple lines, identical in substance), `{error ? <Notice tone="error">{error}</Notice> : null}` is exact at :504, and I counted the 28 keys in EMPTY_VALUES at :220-249. `grep -n "scrollIntoView\|useRef\|\.focus()\|autoFocus" students/new/page.tsx` returns nothing. The failure is reachable — students.validation.js:127-128 requires only `first_name` and `admission_date`, both near the top, and any format rejection (email, phone) produces a details array that binds entirely to rendered controls, and `setSaving(false)` at :464 leaves the button looking idle.

### The status filter hardcodes `bg-white` while its text colour is the theme token `text-ink`, so in dark mode it is near-white text on a white box. — `(school)/school/parents/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 258
- **What a user sees:** In dark mode the "All parents / Active / Deactivated" filter is slate-100 text on #fff — the user cannot read which state is selected.
- **Evidence:** parents/page.tsx:258 `className="rounded-md border border-border-strong bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink"`. globals.css:251-253 `:root[data-theme="dark"] { --ink: var(--slate-100); ... }` and :201-205 the same under `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root:not([data-theme="light"]) }`; `--color-ink: var(--ink)` at globals.css:301 is what `text-ink` resolves to. components/theme.tsx:46-52 writes/removes `data-theme` on the root. The search input immediately above, at :243, uses `className="field-input mt-0 max-w-xs"` correctly.
- **Fix:** Replace the hand-rolled class string with `className="field-select"`, which carries the tokenised background, border and focus ring.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The class string is verbatim at :258. `grep -n "white" app/globals.css` returns only `white-space: nowrap;` at :464 — nothing redefines `--color-white`, so `bg-white` is Tailwind's literal #fff while `--ink` flips to `var(--slate-100)` at both :203 and :252. globals.css:593-602 shows `.field-input, .field-select, .field-textarea { ... background: var(--surface-1); color: var(--ink); }`, so the same file already contains the themed alternative and the sibling control at :243 uses it. Two controls in one row, one legible in dark mode and one not.

### `CONTROL_CLASS` hardcodes `bg-white` with `text-ink`, making the sole filter on the screen unreadable in dark mode. — `(school)/school/results/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 195
- **What a user sees:** In dark mode the publication filter — the only control on the screen — is near-white text on white, so the user cannot read which publication state is applied to the table below it.
- **Evidence:** results/page.tsx:194-196 `const CONTROL_CLASS = 'rounded-md border border-border-strong bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none ' + 'focus:border-teal';`, applied at :365 `className={CONTROL_CLASS}`. `--ink` becomes `var(--slate-100)` under `:root[data-theme="dark"]` (globals.css:252) and under the `prefers-color-scheme: dark` block (:203); `--color-ink: var(--ink)` at :301 is what `text-ink` compiles to. Theme switching is live — components/theme.tsx:46-52.
- **Fix:** Use `field-select` from globals.css instead of the literal class string.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Verbatim at :194-196 and applied at :365. Same verification as the parents finding: no `--color-white` override exists anywhere in globals.css, `--ink` is redefined to slate-100 in both dark branches, and `.field-select` at globals.css:594-602 plus :657-667 already provides a themed background, colour, 3px focus ring and a dark-mode chevron. This one is worse than the parents case because the screen has exactly one control.

### The interface docblock asserts MySQL returns DECIMAL as a string, which `dialectOptions.decimalNumbers = true` contradicts. — `frontend/src/app/(school)/school/teachers/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `stale-comment` · line 71
- **What a user sees:** Nothing renders wrong on this screen — the union type plus `Number(value)` absorbs either shape. The defect is a false factual claim in a file written as an exemplar, in exactly the bug class this project has already been burned by.
- **Evidence:** teachers/page.tsx:70-74 is verbatim as reported, including ` * driver returns \`DECIMAL\` as a **string** to avoid the precision loss a float round-trip causes —` and ` * so this arrives as \`"7.50"\`, not \`7.5\`.` backend/src/config/database.js:57-59 is `  dialectOptions: {` / `    // Return DECIMAL as string-free numbers where safe; money is handled via the money util.` / `    decimalNumbers: true,`, and line 42 of that file is indeed `  dialect: 'mysql',` — the line the comment cites for its false claim. frontend/src/lib/money.ts:14 is ` * A DECIMAL does **not** arrive as a string.`
- **Fix:** Correct the docblock to cite database.js:59 (`decimalNumbers: true`) and narrow the field to `experience_years: number | null`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Every citation checked out to the exact line. database.js:42 really is `dialect: 'mysql',` and 57-59 really is the decimalNumbers block, so the docblock's premise is disproven by the file it cites. Impact is on maintainers, not end users — but the claim itself is false, not merely stylistic.

### The list has no row action and there is no teacher detail route anywhere in the app, so `PATCH /teachers/:id` is unreachable and a teacher can never be deactivated after creation. — `frontend/src/app/(school)/school/teachers/page.tsx`

- **What a user sees:** A departing teacher's record cannot be set inactive from any screen, so it keeps consuming `teacher_limit` (POST /teachers carries enforceLimit) and the school cannot add a replacement without buying capacity it is not using.

The only mutation is the create link at 259-266 (`can('teachers.manage') ? (<a href="/school/teachers/new" ...`). `find src/app -name "*.tsx" | grep "\["` returns nothing across all 60 page.tsx files. Backend teachers.routes.js:111-117 mounts `router.patch(` / `'/:id',` / `requirePermission('teachers.manage'),` / `validate({ params: schemas.idParam, body: schemas.update }),`. teachers/new/page.tsx:462-465 states the consequence: "§15.3 names no teacher deletion, so this flag is how a departure is kept."

The fix is also SRS-safe: teachers.validation.js:105 says in its own words "`is_active` and `left_at` are editable here rather than through a dedicated route", with `is_active: fields.is_active,` (118) and `left_at: fields.left_at,` (119) on the update schema — no new column, permission or route needed.

### The status filter offers `transferred` and `left`, but the UI has no route that can produce either — FR-STUDENT-002's three lifecycle endpoints have no screen at all. — `frontend/src/app/(school)/school/students/page.tsx`

- **Status:** Closed — FR-STUDENT-002’s three transitions are on the Students list as a row action gated on `students.progression` (the narrower key: a receptionist holds `manage` and can admit, and cannot decide a child has left). Verified live: a transfer wrote `status=transferred, transfer_to="Riverside Grammar"`, a leaving wrote `status=left, leaving_reason="Family relocated abroad"`, and **the `transferred` filter returned a row for the first time**. Promotion was exercised too and correctly refused — *"A promotion must name a different class"*, surfaced on `class_id`, because the probe school has one class. The actions column shows `—` on a non-active row, since nothing returns a student to `active`.
- **Category:** `ux` · line 111
- **What a user sees:** A holder of `students.progression` cannot promote, transfer or mark a student as left anywhere in the product, while the filter advertises two statuses nothing can ever set — so they return an empty table forever, which the screen's own docblock (lines 106-109) argues is worse than omitting them.
- **Evidence:** students/page.tsx:111 is `const STATUSES = ['active', 'transferred', 'left'] as const;`. students.routes.js mounts `/:id/promote` (89), `/:id/transfer` (98) and `/:id/leave` (106), each `requirePermission('students.progression')`. students.validation.js:80 is `  status: forbiddenField('"status" is set by promote / transfer / leave, not by this request'),` and the service writes `status: STUDENT_STATUS.TRANSFERRED,` (136) and `status: STUDENT_STATUS.LEFT,` (146) only inside those transitions. A grep for transfer/leave/promote callers across `frontend/src` finds no `api.post` and no href.
- **Fix:** Add `/school/students/[id]` with the three actions behind ConfirmDialog, or narrow `STATUSES` to `['active']` until it exists.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Line 111 matches character for character, and the backend confirms status is system-owned: students.validation.js:80's `forbiddenField('"status" is set by promote / transfer / leave, not by this request')` means no create or patch can produce those two values, and no frontend file calls the three routes that can.

'active' : 'inactive'} />,`. subjects.routes.js mounts `router.patch(` `'/:id',` `requirePermission('subjects.manage'),` at 98-103, `router.delete('/:id', …)` at 106, `router.post('/:id/classes', …)` at 52 and `router.post('/:id/teachers', …)` at 75 — none has a frontend caller, and there is no `[id]` route. subjects/new/page.tsx:318-321 promises the follow-up: "Subjects are not soft-deleted, so this flag is the whole of a subject's lifecycle. ### `figure()` defaults to zero decimal places, so DECIMAL(9,2) mark totals go through `toFixed(0)` and half-marks are rounded away. — `(student)/student/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `money` · line 65
- **What a user sees:** A student who scored 437.5 of 500 sees "438 / 500" (`(437.5).toFixed(0) === '438'`), while the school-office screen shows 437.5. The portal rounds up and shows a mark the student did not get.
- **Evidence:** Lines 64-69 are verbatim, including `function figure(value: string | number | null, digits = 0)` and `return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed.toFixed(digits) : null;`. Lines 98-99 call it with no `digits`: `const got = figure(row.total_marks_obtained);` / `const outOf = figure(row.total_full_marks);` (the percentage cell at line 108 does pass 2). `models/exams.js:297-298` are verbatim `total_full_marks: { type: DataTypes.DECIMAL(9, 2), ... }` / `total_marks_obtained: { type: DataTypes.DECIMAL(9, 2), ... }`, and fractional marks are ordinary input: `exams.validation.js:44` is `const markField = Joi.number().min(0).max(99999.99).precision(2);`, used by `marks_obtained` at :256. The sibling school screen formats the same columns with `(school)/school/results/page.tsx:152`: `const MARKS = new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });`
- **Fix:** Format the marks cells with the same `Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })` the school results screen uses, or at minimum pass 2 to `figure` for both.
- **Why it survived refutation:** `digits = 0` is the declared default at line 65 and the two mark calls at 98-99 pass nothing; half-marks are reachable because the mark field is `Joi.number()...precision(2)`, not an integer.

### The "nothing assigned" empty state is gated only on subjects and classes, so a section-only teacher is told they have no assignments while the metric card above says otherwise, and their section list is hidden. — `(teacher)/teacher/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `ux` · line 100
- **What a user sees:** A teacher who is section teacher of two sections but teaches no subject sees "Section teacher of: 2" and, directly beneath, "You have no subjects or classes assigned yet." The panel naming those two sections is suppressed, so the dashboard contradicts itself and hides the only assignment they have.
- **Evidence:** Lines 97-100 are verbatim: `<MetricCard label="Section teacher of" value={data.counts.sectionTeacherOf} />` then `</dl>` then `{data.counts.subjects === 0 && data.counts.classes === 0 ? (`, and the branch renders the "You have no subjects or classes assigned yet." paragraph instead of the three panels (lines 106-129). Backend `teachers.service.js:393-399` is verbatim: `const subjectIds = [...new Set(subjects.map((r) => Number(r.subject_id)).filter(Boolean))];` and `classIds` built from `subjects.map((r) => Number(r.class_id))` plus `classTeacherOf.map((r) => Number(r.id))` — sections contribute to neither count. `sectionTeacherOf` comes from `db.Section.findAll({ where: { class_teacher_id: teacher.id, school_id: teacher.school_id }, ... })` (teachers.service.js:357-361), and `class_teacher_id` on a section is writable through `PATCH /classes/:id/sections/:sectionId` (`classes.routes.js:58-64`, `schemas.updateSection`; `classes.validation.js` carries `class_teacher_id` on the section schemas).
- **Fix:** Gate on all three counts: `data.counts.subjects === 0 && data.counts.classes === 0 && data.counts.sectionTeacherOf === 0`. The `.filter(([, rows]) => (rows as NamedRow[]).length > 0)` at line 112 already hides the empty panels.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The condition at line 100 names only `counts.subjects` and `counts.classes`, and the service's `classIds` set (line 394-399) is built from `subjects`' `class_id` and `classTeacherOf` ids only — a section assignment raises neither count, so the contradictory state is reachable. The finding's path label is loose (`PATCH /classes/:id/sections` vs the mounted `/:id/sections/:sectionId`), but that is prose, not a quotation.

### The register has no row action and no edit route exists anywhere in the app, so `PATCH /timetable/:id` is unreachable and a wrongly-scheduled period can never be corrected or its slot freed. — `(school)/school/timetable/page.tsx`

- **Status:** Closed — `/school/timetable/[id]` on `PATCH /timetable/:id`. Verified live: period 3 → 4, teacher assigned, room changed. The `is_active` control says in words that retiring a slot does **not** free the period, which is what `timetable.routes.js` states and what an operator would otherwise discover from a 409.
- **Category:** `ux` · line 178
- **What a user sees:** An administrator who mistypes a period number or room cannot fix it: no edit control, no delete, and no way to retire the row either (the status select exists only on the create form). The slot stays claimed by `timetables_section_day_period_unique`, and later attempts to schedule it 409 against a row the operator cannot reach.
- **Evidence:** The columns array declares exactly the seven keys quoted — grep gives `181: key: 'day'`, `186: 'period'`, `216: 'time'`, `230: 'class'`, `258: 'subject'`, `284: 'teacher'`, `298: 'room'` — and no actions column. Lines 425-430 are verbatim `<DataTable columns={columns} rows={rows} rowKey={(row) => row.id} caption="Timetable entries" />` with no row link. `ls -R` on the route directory returns only `page.tsx` and `new/page.tsx`; a tree-wide grep for `timetable` in `frontend/src` returns only nav entries (`lib/nav.ts:158,209`), prose, this register and its create form — no `[id]` segment. Backend `timetable.routes.js:119-125` mounts `router.patch('/:id', requirePermission('timetable.manage'), validate({ params: schemas.idParam, body: schemas.update }), ...)`, and the file has no `router.delete` at all; its header at :37 reads "## No DELETE, and `is_active` does not free a slot". The create form promises the edit at :846-850, verbatim: "the period stays claimed until this entry is edited."
- **Fix:** Add an actions column gated on `can('timetable.manage')` linking to a `timetable/[id]` edit form on `PATCH /timetable/:id`. `schemas.update` is `Joi.object({...}).min(1)` (timetable.validation.js:92-109) with no `.required()` keys, so the create form's fields and its `timeOrdered`/`teachingSlotNeedsSubject` promotion can be reused as-is.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Verified three ways: the directory listing contains only `new/`, the DataTable call at 425-430 passes no row action, and `router.patch('/:id', ...)` is mounted at timetable.routes.js:119 with no DELETE anywhere in the file. `timetable.validation.js:109` is `}).min(1);`, confirming the fix is viable.

### The forced-password-change screen is a dead end: no sign-out control and no link off the page, even though the backend allow-lists logout for exactly this state. — `src/app/(auth)/change-password/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 120
- **What a user sees:** A user who signed in as the wrong account, or who cannot satisfy the server's password policy, has no navigation and no sign-out; every other API call 403s with PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED. Their escape is clearing cookies or closing the browser. The backend built the exit; the UI never exposes it.
- **Evidence:** The whole render is AuthCard + form, change-password/page.tsx:72-120, ending `<SubmitButton busy={submitting} busyLabel="Saving…">` / 'Change password' / `</form>` / `</AuthCard>`. I grepped the file for anchors: it is the only page in (auth) with no `<a href`, no Link and no logout button. Every sibling has an exit — login/page.tsx:101, forgot-password/page.tsx:71 and :105, reset-password/page.tsx:79 and :101, verify-email/page.tsx:79 and :102. The backend kept the door open for this state: auth.routes.js:115-116 is `/** FR-AUTH-002. Allow-listed in `enforcePasswordChange` — a user must be able to walk away. */` above `protectedRoutes.post('/logout', requireCsrfToken(), asyncHandler(controller.logout));`, and enforcePasswordChange (authenticate.js) refuses everything not in its allow-list. AppShell, which carries the sign-out button (shell.tsx:187 `logout().then(() => router.replace('/login'));`), is not rendered here.
- **Fix:** Add a secondary 'Sign out' button under the submit that calls useAuth().logout() then router.replace('/login') — the one other call this state permits.
- **Why it survived refutation:** I read the whole file: lines 72-120 are exactly the render described and there is no anchor, Link or button other than the submit. The grep for `<a href` across the five auth pages returned hits in all four siblings at precisely the cited lines (forgot-password 71/105, reset-password 79/101, verify-email 79/102, login 101) and none in change-password. auth.routes.js:115-116 carries that comment verbatim above the logout mount, so the allow-list is deliberate and unused by the UI. One nuance worth knowing before implementing: the logout route is CSRF-guarded, so the sign-out button inherits the stale-CSRF defect above and would 403 server-side until that is fixed — the client would still clear and route to /login (auth.tsx:200-213).

### A malformed verification token renders the developer string 'Validation failed' to the user, and the comment beside it claims one message covers every failure. — `src/app/(auth)/verify-email/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 49
- **What a user sees:** A user whose verification link was wrapped or truncated by their mail client sees a red box containing only the words 'Validation failed', with no indication that the problem is the link.
- **Evidence:** verify-email/page.tsx:47-51 is verbatim `setMessage(caught instanceof ApiError ? caught.message : 'Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.');`, shown at line 96 as `<Notice tone="error">{message}</Notice>`. For a well-formed but unknown or expired token the service message is human — auth.service.js verifyEmail: `throw ApiError.badRequest('This verification link is not valid or has expired.', { code: 'VERIFICATION_TOKEN_INVALID' });`. For a token failing the shape rule (auth.validation.js:148 `verifyEmail: Joi.object({ token: singleUseToken })`, min 20 / max 200 / /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/), the request never reaches the service: validate.js raises ApiError.validation, and ApiError.js:66-68 is `static validation(message = 'Validation failed', details) { return new ApiError(422, message, { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', details }); }`. errorHandler.js:265 sends `message: apiError.expose ? apiError.message : 'Internal server error'`, so 'Validation failed' reaches the client verbatim. The comment at lines 91-95 asserts 'One message covers an unknown token, a used one and an expired one — the service gives a single code for all three' — true of those three, but a fourth case carries VALIDATION_ERROR and a message written for a developer.
- **Fix:** Branch on the code: for caught.code === 'VALIDATION_ERROR' (or status 422) show the page's own 'That link is incomplete' copy; keep echoing the service's message for 400/VERIFICATION_TOKEN_INVALID.
- **Why it survived refutation:** All four quotes verified in place: the setMessage block at 47-51, the Notice at 96, ApiError.validation at ApiError.js:66-68, and the verifyEmail schema using singleUseToken at auth.validation.js:148. I also checked errorHandler.js:265, which passes the message through for exposed (client) errors, so nothing rewrites 'Validation failed' before it reaches the Notice. The path is reachable by the same mangled-link case the page's own no-token copy names.

### The only required field on the form is marked with an `aria-hidden` asterisk and carries no `required` or `aria-required`, so a screen reader is never told it is required. — `frontend/src/app/(platform)/super-admin/invoices/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — found already fixed when re-checked against the tree; the status line had not been updated
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 404
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user hears "Subscription, combo box" with no indication that leaving it blank is the one thing guaranteed to fail; the asterisk a sighted user sees is explicitly hidden from assistive tech.
- **Evidence:** page.tsx:403-411 `<label htmlFor="subscription_id" className="block text-sm font-medium">` / `Subscription <span aria-hidden="true">*</span>` / `<select id="subscription_id" value={values.subscription_id} onChange={set('subscription_id')} aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors.subscription_id)} className={INPUT_CLASS}>`. The form is `noValidate` (page.tsx:401). `grep -n required` on the file returns only prose lines 16/208/286 — no `required` attribute anywhere. components/form.tsx:16-17 "**A required field says so in words, not with a red asterisk alone.**", implemented in `FieldLabel` (`<span …>required</span>`) and `SelectField` (`required={required}` on the `<select>`); `SelectField` is not used here.
- **Fix:** Replace the hand-rolled div/label/select with `<SelectField id="subscription_id" label="Subscription" required …>` from @/components/form.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Lines 403-404 are verbatim (`Subscription <span aria-hidden="true">*</span>`), and `grep -n "aria-invalid|aria-describedby|required"` over the whole file shows `aria-invalid` only at line 410, no `aria-describedby` at all, and no `required` attribute on any control — so nothing exposes the required state. form.tsx:14-17 and the `FieldLabel`/`SelectField` implementations (required word, `required={required}`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-describedby`) are exactly as quoted.

- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user who submits without choosing gets `"discount_type" is required` as detached text beside a select that still reports itself valid; sighted users see no required marker on the one required control the file hand-rolls, while its siblings all carry one.
- **Evidence:** page.tsx:429-449 `<label htmlFor="discount_type" className="block text-sm font-medium">` / `Discount type` / `<select id="discount_type" required value={values.discount_type} onChange={set('discount_type')} className={INPUT_CLASS}>` … `{fieldErrors.discount_type ? (<p className="mt-1 text-sm text-danger">{fieldErrors.discount_type}</p>) : null}`. Same shape at :411-427 (description textarea), :563-583 (status select), :185-218 (RestrictionField's `<select multiple>`) — none set `aria-invalid` or `aria-describedby`. Sibling fields use `Field required` (:393-401, :451-469), which renders the word "required" via form.tsx's FieldLabel.

One correction to the evidence: the claim that "the `required` attribute is inert" because the form is `noValidate` is wrong — `noValidate` suppresses constraint validation, not the accessibility mapping, so line 435's `required` does expose the required state to assistive tech; what is missing is the visible marker its sibling `Field required` controls render and the error association.

(` / `<p className="text-sm text-muted">Loading…</p>`, and :264-269 is verbatim `<DataTable` / `columns={columns}` / `rows={rows}` / `rowKey={(row) => row.id}` / `caption="Organizations"` / `/>` with no `busy`. table.tsx declares `busy?: boolean;` at :161 and dims in place at :171-172; its header at :12-15 names this exact pattern as the defect the prop replaced. Neither `busy` nor `LoadingBlock` appears anywhere in organizations/page.tsx.
- **Fix:** Render `LoadingBlock` only on the first load (`loading && rows.length === 0`) and pass `busy={loading}` to DataTable, as payments/page.tsx:393 and :418 do.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Both quotes are verbatim at the stated lines and the DataTable call really does omit the prop that table.tsx documents as the fix for this exact behaviour.

### The list can create organizations but never change one: no row action, no detail route, and the status filter's stated purpose has no matching control. — `E:/School Managment System/frontend/src/app/(platform)/super-admin/organizations/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 222
- **What a user sees:** A Super Admin can find the one suspended organization the filter was built to surface and then has nothing to do about it. Suspending, reactivating or fixing a typo in an organization is impossible from the product; `notes` and `address` can be written on create and never read back.
- **Evidence:** organizations/page.tsx:215-217 is verbatim ("Status earns a control where the other parameters do not: `resolveTenant` refuses a suspended..."), and :159 is verbatim `{ key: 'status', header: 'Status', cell: (row) => <StatusBadge status={row.status} /> },`. No column carries an edit affordance and `grep -rn 'organizations/' frontend/src --include=*.tsx` shows the only organizations URL constructed anywhere is the `/new` link — nothing calls `PATCH /organizations/:id`. There is no `[id]` directory under frontend/src/app. Backend organizations.routes.js:60-61 mounts `router.get('/:id', requirePermission('organizations.view'), ...)` and :67-68 `router.patch('/:id', requirePlatformScope(), requirePermission('organizations.manage'), ...)`, and organizations.validation.js `update` accepts name, code, email, phone, address, website, status and notes with `.min(1)`.
- **Fix:** Add a row action — an edit modal (SelectField for status plus the other seven fields) calling `PATCH /organizations/${id}`, gated on `can('organizations.manage')`, following the payments ReviewDialog pattern.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The two quotes are verbatim and the backend PATCH/GET routes exist as quoted with no frontend caller. One correction: the evidence's aside that "no column renders a link" is inaccurate — the website column at lines 146-156 renders an `<a href={row.website} target="_blank">` — but that is an outbound link, not a row action, so the substantive claim stands.

### The status select and the notes textarea are hand-rolled instead of using the shared field components, so their validation errors are not programmatically associated with the control. — `E:/School Managment System/frontend/src/app/(platform)/super-admin/organizations/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 209
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user who submits over-long notes hears nothing: the field is not announced invalid and the error text is an unlinked paragraph. Eight fields on one form behave two different ways.
- **Evidence:** organizations/new/page.tsx:206-220 is verbatim, including `<select id="status" value={values.status} onChange={set('status')} className={INPUT_CLASS}>` and `{fieldErrors.status ? (` / `<p className="mt-1 text-sm text-danger">{fieldErrors.status}</p>`, with the identical shape for the notes textarea at 224-236. Neither control carries `aria-invalid` or `aria-describedby`, and neither error `<p>` has an id. components/form.tsx `SelectField` sets `aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}` and `aria-describedby={error ? errorId : hint ? hintId : undefined}` and `className={`field-select ${className ?? ''}`}`; `TextAreaField` does the same with `field-textarea`. The six `Field` components at 156-203 get that wiring. INPUT_CLASS at 50-52 is verbatim and ends `'field-input '`, while globals.css:656-663 gives only `.field-select` the `appearance: none` and the drawn chevron. The notes path is reachable: the textarea sets no maxLength, and the schema is `notes: Joi.string().trim().max(5000)...`.
- **Fix:** Replace both blocks with `SelectField` and `TextAreaField` from components/form.tsx, passing `error={fieldErrors.status}` / `error={fieldErrors.notes}`; INPUT_CLASS can then be deleted.
- **Why it survived refutation:** I read both hand-rolled blocks and both shared components: the aria attributes are present in form.tsx and absent on this page, and the >5000-character notes path makes the missing association reachable rather than theoretical.

### The revenue cards' period hints print raw ISO-8601 timestamps because the API sends Date objects, not the calendar dates the interface implies. — `E:/School Managment System/frontend/src/app/(platform)/super-admin/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 138
- **What a user sees:** The two most-read cards on the Super Admin's first screen carry a hint like '2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z → 2026-09-30T23:59:59.999Z' — a hint added so the figure is checkable, in the one form a non-engineer cannot read.
- **Evidence:** super-admin/page.tsx:135-144 is verbatim, including `hint={data.period ? `${data.period.month.from} → ${data.period.month.to}` : undefined}` and the same for `year`. platform.service.js returns verbatim `period: { month: { from: monthly.from, to: monthly.to }, year: { from: yearly.from, to: yearly.to } },` where `monthly = periodRange('monthly', at)`; utils/dates.js `periodRange` returns `{ from: startOfMonth(reference), to: endOfMonth(reference) }` and both are built with `new Date(Date.UTC(...))` — `endOfMonth` is `new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999))`. ApiResponse.ok ends in `res.status(...).json(body)`, so `Date.toJSON()` yields the ISO string; the interface at page.tsx:32-35 types them `string` and interpolates them raw.
- **Fix:** Format both bounds with `Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })`, as payments/page.tsx:110 already constructs. Keep timeZone UTC, since the service computes the range in UTC.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The MetricCard hints are verbatim, and I traced periodRange → startOfMonth/endOfMonth → `new Date(Date.UTC(...))` and ApiResponse.ok's `res.json`, which serialises a Date to ISO with no intervening formatter.

### The Readiness warning text uses raw Tailwind `text-amber-700` instead of the themed `text-warn`, failing contrast on the dark surface — while the success case in the same column correctly uses a theme token. — `(platform)/super-admin/plans/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 198
- **What a user sees:** In dark mode the three states that need attention — 'No active price', 'Not offered', 'Sellable · N limits unset' — sit at roughly 3.4:1 on the row and 3.8:1 on the page ground, below AA for 14px text, while the reassuring 'Sellable' is bright and legible.
- **Evidence:** plans/page.tsx:198 and :212 both `<span className="whitespace-nowrap text-amber-700">`, against :220 `return <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-success">Sellable</span>;`. globals.css:330 `--color-success: var(--success);` with the dark block redefining `--success: #47cd89;` at :231, while `--color-warn` is already exported at :332 and `--warn: #fdb022;` redefined at :234. `text-amber-700` resolves to Tailwind's own `--color-amber-700` (globals.css uses `@theme inline` at :300, which adds tokens without resetting the default palette, and its own `--amber-700: #93370d` at :79 is not in that namespace).
- **Fix:** Replace `text-amber-700` with `text-warn` on both branches, matching the `text-success` branch below.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Both warning spans and the `text-success` span are verbatim at plans/page.tsx:198, :212, :220. Tailwind is v4.3.3 (package.json:24) and globals.css:300 opens `@theme inline` with no `--color-*: initial`, so the default amber palette is live and amber-700 (oklch 55.5% 0.163 48.998, the converted #b45309) is what renders. I computed the ratio against both candidate grounds — 3.44:1 on `--surface-1` #161b24 (globals.css:48, 209) and 3.77:1 on `--paper` #0d1117 — both under 4.5:1. Reachable by one click on the shell's ThemeToggle (components/shell.tsx:241). The themed token exists and is used two branches down in the same cell, so this is inconsistency inside one function, not a palette that was never available.

### The description textarea and the two selects are hand-rolled instead of using TextAreaField/SelectField, so their validation errors carry no id and the controls carry no aria-invalid or aria-describedby. — `(platform)/super-admin/plans/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 210
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user who submits an invalid description, visibility or renewal mode is moved to the field and hears only its label — the server's per-field 422 is a detached paragraph with no id, never associated and never announced. Sighted keyboard users get no red border either, because globals.css:644-648 `.field-input[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: var(--danger); }` cannot match a control that never gets the attribute.
- **Evidence:** new/page.tsx:210-218 verbatim (`<textarea id="description" rows={4} value={values.description} onChange={set('description')} className={INPUT_CLASS} />` then `{fieldErrors.description ? (<p className="mt-1 text-sm text-danger">{fieldErrors.description}</p>`), the same shape for `visibility` at :230-245 and `default_renewal_mode` at :283-298, and a hand-rolled checkbox at :334-345. components/form.tsx:106-107 shows the guarantee they miss: `aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}` / `aria-describedby={error ? errorId : hint ? hintId : undefined}`, stated as a contract at form.tsx:14-15. TextAreaField (form.tsx:171), SelectField (:126) and CheckboxField (:210) exist and are unused here.
- **Fix:** Swap the three blocks and the checkbox for TextAreaField, SelectField (x2) and CheckboxField, passing `error={fieldErrors.x}` and `hint=` as the `Field` calls above them already do.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Every quoted block is verbatim (cited line numbers drift by 2-5). I checked the escape hatch and it is closed: INPUT_CLASS does include the `field-input` class, so the aria-invalid rule at globals.css:644-646 is present and applicable — it simply never matches, because no `aria-invalid` attribute is ever written on these three controls, and the error `<p>` has no `id` for any `aria-describedby` to point at. The three shared primitives that supply both exist in components/form.tsx and are imported from the same module this file already imports `Field`, `Notice` and `SubmitButton` from (new/page.tsx:54).

### On all three plan-scoped screens the error state is never cleared when the plan picker is set back to 'Choose a plan…', so the ErrorNotice is stuck. — `(platform)/super-admin/plans/features/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `missing-state` · line 115
- **What a user sees:** An operator on a stale link like /super-admin/plans/limits?plan=42 for a deleted plan gets 'Something went wrong'. Selecting 'Choose a plan…' does not clear it — the red panel stays and the 'Choose a plan above to see its limits' state is unreachable, because `!selected` is only tested after `error`. Only picking a different valid plan or reloading escapes.
- **Evidence:** components/planScope.tsx:114-123 verbatim: `useEffect(() => { if (!planId) { setDetail(null); return undefined; }` followed by `setLoading(true); setError(null); setRefusal(null);` — the resets are all after the early return. The three screens order the error branch ahead of the 'no plan chosen' branch (features/page.tsx:112-119, limits/page.tsx:142-149, modules/page.tsx:100-107). The error is reachable: plans.service.js:329 `if (!plan) throw ApiError.notFound('Plan not found', { code: 'PLAN_NOT_FOUND' });` and PLAN_NOT_FOUND is absent from EXPLAINED_CODES (lib/useCollection.ts:75-99), so it lands in `error`.
- **Fix:** Clear the transient state before the early return: `if (!planId) { setDetail(null); setError(null); setRefusal(null); setLoading(false); return undefined; }` and/or test `!selected` before `error`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** planScope.tsx:114-123 and the three branch ladders are verbatim, and I traced the escape: `select('')` (planScope.tsx:91-96) deletes the `plan` param, `selected` becomes null, the effect takes the early return, `error` is never touched, and the render ladder hits `error ?` before `!selected ?`. The stuck panel is real. One half of the stated evidence is wrong and should not be repeated: 'reload only bumps the nonce, which re-runs this effect and hits the same early return' is false — with `?plan=42` still set, `planId` is truthy, so Try again does re-issue the request; it just fails identically every time because the plan is gone. The defect is the uncleared state, not a dead retry.

### Free-text report parameters are un-debounced, so every keystroke fires a full report query. — `(platform)/super-admin/reports/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 557
- **What a user sees:** Typing USD into the Fee report's Currency box sends three /reports/fees requests; the two intermediate ones filter on a partial code and come back all-zero, so the screen shows a school with no fees between keystrokes, and each keystroke spends the pre-auth rate-limit budget on a grouped SUM over student_fees.
- **Evidence:** reports/page.tsx:548-556: `<input id={param.key} type={param.kind === 'date' ? 'date' : 'text'} value={params[param.key] ?? ''} onChange={(event) => setParams((prev) => ({ ...prev, [param.key]: event.target.value }))}`. `params` feeds the `query` memo (326-334) which is a dependency of the read effect (383: `}, [allowed, missing.length, spec.path, query, nonce]);`). `grep -n setTimeout` on the file returns nothing. The exemplar states the rule this breaks — schools/page.tsx:19-21: "`apiLimiter` is mounted before authentication, so an unthrottled request per keystroke spends a real budget — and `q` reaches a `LIKE` scan. 300 ms is long enough to collapse a typed word into one request".
- **Fix:** Debounce the text params by the same 300 ms the list screens use before folding them into `query`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The intermediate-request claim holds because the schema does not reject a partial code: reports.validation.js:88 `currency: Joi.string().trim().uppercase().max(10),` accepts 'U' and 'US', and reports.service.js:211 `if (query.currency) where.currency = query.currency;` then filters on it, so rows is empty and billed/collected fall to 0 (reports.service.js:235-237). app.js:283 `app.use(apiLimiter);` is a global mount ahead of the routers, so the rate-limit half is right too.

### The seven report tabs convey which one is selected by background colour alone, with no aria-pressed/aria-current, and the explanation for a disabled tab lives only in a title. — `(platform)/super-admin/reports/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 484
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user hears seven plain buttons with no selected state, so nothing says which report is on screen; a user missing fees.view gets a greyed Fees button whose "Requires …" reason is in a title on an unfocusable control and so is never announced.
- **Evidence:** reports/page.tsx:480-496, verbatim: `<button key={entry.type} type="button" onClick={() => { setSelected(entry.type); setParams({}); }} disabled={!reachable} title={reachable ? entry.summary : `Requires ${entry.permissions.join(' and ')}`} className={`rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm transition-colors ${entry.type === selected ? 'border-teal bg-teal text-white' : 'border-border-strong hover:border-teal disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50'}`}>{entry.label}</button>`. There is no aria-pressed, aria-current, role="tab"/aria-selected anywhere in the block, and a disabled button is not focusable.
- **Fix:** Add aria-pressed={entry.type === selected} (or a real tablist/aria-selected pattern) and put the "Requires …" text in visible or sr-only copy.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The quoted block is verbatim at 480-496 and carries no ARIA state attribute of any kind, so the selected tab is machine-indistinguishable from the other six. One sub-claim is weak: the selected tab differs by fill and text colour together ('bg-teal text-white' versus an outline), which a colour-blind user can still separate by luminance — the defect is the missing programmatic state and the title-on-a-disabled-button, not colour alone.

### The School and Status controls are hand-rolled selects whose required state is an aria-hidden asterisk and whose error text is never associated with the control, although SelectField already solves both. — `(platform)/super-admin/principals/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 334
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user hears "School, combo box" with nothing marking it mandatory — the only indicator is hidden from them by aria-hidden — and when the server rejects the field the message is not tied to the control. Status has the same gap.

The select (357-363) carries id, value, onChange, disabled and className only — no required, aria-required, aria-invalid or aria-describedby. The messages below have no ids to point at: :386 `<p className="mt-1 text-sm text-danger">{fieldErrors.school_id}</p>`, :399-401 the truncation note, :423 the status error.
- **Fix:** Replace both hand-rolled blocks with SelectField.
- **Why it survived refutation:** grep for `required` in principals/new/page.tsx returns hits only on the five `Field` components (285, 295, 305, 316) and prose — the school_id select at 357-363 has none, so the aria-hidden asterisk at 334 really is the only required marker. components/form.tsx:16-17 states the rule ("**A required field says so in words, not with a red asterisk alone.** An asterisk is a convention, not a label") and SelectField (form.tsx:126-161) wires FieldLabel required, required, aria-invalid and aria-describedby. principals.validation.js:73 `school_id: fields.school_id.required(),` confirms the server requires it.

### The required Organization select loads one page of 100 and throws away the total, so on a larger platform the organization simply is not in the list and nothing says so. — `(platform)/super-admin/schools/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 139
- **What a user sees:** With more than 100 organizations the one the Super Admin needs can be absent from the only control that sets a required foreign key, with no on-screen message; the operator concludes the organization was never created.

The screen renders no truncation notice and offers no filter (272-288 shows only the error/empty/hint branches).

The sibling screen does exactly it — principals/new/page.tsx:160-172 uses api.page and keeps result.meta.total, rendering at :400 "Showing ${schools.length} of ${schoolTotal} schools — a page holds at most ${SCHOOL_LIMIT}. Filter above to reach the rest." Note the limit is disclosed in this page's own header (49-57), just not to the user.

### The Schools list is read-only with no row action and no detail route, so five of the nine §9.2 operations have no reachable UI. — `(platform)/super-admin/schools/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — found already fixed when re-checked against the tree; the status line had not been updated

- **What a user sees:** Once a school is created it can never be edited, activated, suspended, archived, deleted, given a Principal, or have its usage inspected from the UI.

'schools.usage.view')` (FR-SADMIN-008) — and the grep above shows no frontend caller for any of them. The fix reuses existing permission keys, so it invents nothing.

### The callout panel hardcodes Tailwind's fixed amber palette, so in dark theme light text sits on a near-white background. — `(platform)/super-admin/settings/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 61

The dark block at :251-255 is exactly `:root[data-theme="dark"] { --ink: var(--slate-100); --ink-soft: …; --muted: var(--slate-400); }` with `--slate-100: #f1f3f7` (:39) and `--slate-400: #98a1b3` (:43) over `--paper: var(--slate-950)` = #0d1117 (:49). Blending amber-50 at 60% over #0d1117 gives ≈#9E9D96: 2.4:1 against #f1f3f7 and 1.04:1 against #98a1b3, matching the claim. Dark theme is user-reachable — components/theme.tsx:51 `root.setAttribute('data-theme', choice)` — and the themed alternative really exists (`--warn-soft`/`--warn-border` at :283-285).

### The price dropdown omits the tier-band columns that are part of a price's identity, so a Student-Based plan renders several identical options. — `(platform)/super-admin/subscriptions/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)

- **What a user sees:** Several options reading "monthly · student based · USD"; the operator cannot tell which enrolment band they are billing the school at.

- **Why it survived refutation:** The option block is verbatim at 443-452 and `PriceOption` (:116-124) really lists only id, billing_cycle, cycle_days, pricing_model, currency, is_active, is_default. plans.validation.js:290-308 states "A plan may legitimately carry several rows for one billing cycle — that is how §10.4's Student-Based bands are stored" and its `key` is `[price.billing_cycle, price.cycle_days ?? '', price.pricing_model, price.tier_min_units ?? '', price.tier_max_units ?? ''].join('|')`, so two rows differing only by band are legal and two rows identical in the tuple are refused — i.e. identical-looking options can ONLY be distinct bands. models/subscription.js:130-131 has both columns, and plans.service.js `DETAIL_INCLUDE` (:84-93) puts `prices` on the wire with no `attributes` restriction, while controller `show` (:45-48) returns `present(plan)` — so the tier values already reach this dropdown. Reachability is stronger than the finding claims, not weaker: no screen in the frontend can create a plan_prices row at all (plans/new/page.tsx:26-27 — "prices (FR-SUB-006) … lives behind its own `PUT` … A price control here would invent an endpoint"), and there are no price seeders, so every price in a real deployment was configured through the API, bands included. Billing follows the chosen row: `computeCycleAmount()` uses that row's `base_amount`/`unit_amount` and the columns are copied denormalised onto the subscription.

### All five hand-rolled selects omit aria-invalid and aria-describedby, so their 422 field errors are never announced at the control. — `(platform)/super-admin/subscriptions/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 368
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user tabbing back after a 422 hears "School, combo box" with no indication the field failed; the hint paragraphs are unreachable the same way.
- **Evidence:** page.tsx:368-374 `<select id="school_id" required value={values.school_id} onChange={set('school_id')} className={INPUT_CLASS}>` with the error as a detached sibling at :390-392.
- **Fix:** Replace the five blocks with `SelectField` from `@/components/form`, passing `error` and `hint`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** The select at 368-374 and the detached error at 390-392 are verbatim, and the same shape repeats for plan_id (:405), plan_price_id (:433), billing_cycle (:~473) and renewal_mode (:558) — none carries an aria attribute of any kind. `INPUT_CLASS` at 65-67 really ends `'field-input '`. The primitive does what the finding says, though at 146-151 rather than the cited 139-145: `<select id={id} name={id} required={required} aria-invalid={Boolean(error)} aria-describedby={error ? errorId : hint ? hintId : undefined} className={`field-select ${className ?? ''}`}`, and form.tsx:14-15 states the guarantee "so the message reaches a screen reader at the field rather than as a banner somewhere else." `.field-select` exists (globals.css:601). Only the line citation for form.tsx is loose; the defect and the fix are sound and violate no SRS constraint.

### The screen declares itself a work queue and filters to the rows needing action, then offers no action on any row and no route to one. — `(platform)/super-admin/subscriptions/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — found already fixed when re-checked against the tree; the status line had not been updated
- **Category:** `ux` · line 189
- **What a user sees:** The rows the copy says the system will not handle have nothing to click, and every lifecycle endpoint is unreachable from the UI.
- **Evidence:** page.tsx:8-11 calls itself a work queue; `columns` (189-284) has six read-only cells and no action cell; DataTable at 378-383 gets no row link; no detail route exists.
- **Fix:** A per-row action guarded by `can('subscriptions.lifecycle')` with a ConfirmDialog, or a detail route; otherwise stop calling it a queue.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Line 8 reads "is a directory — you arrive knowing a name. This screen is a work queue: FR-SUB-015's renewal is" and 9 "*\"initiated by the system at cycle end\"*", verbatim. `const columns = useMemo<Column<SubscriptionRow>[]>(` is at 189 and closes `[nameFor]` at 284 with cells school/plan/state/amount/period_end/renewal only. `find . -name "page.tsx"` under src/app returns exactly 60 routes, and the only subscription ones are `subscriptions/page.tsx` and `subscriptions/new/page.tsx` — no `[id]`. A repo-wide grep of frontend/src for `/renew`, `/activate`, `/suspend`, `/reactivate`, `/pause`, `/resume`, `/cancel`, `/upgrade`, `/downgrade` matches nothing but a comment in addons/page.tsx:13, so no screen calls any of them. The route citations are exact: renew at subscriptions.routes.js:246-252, the six transitions at 166-219, upgrade/downgrade at 223-242. Worth noting for the fix: `DataTableProps` (table.tsx:155-162) has no row-link prop at all, so the per-row-action variant is the one that needs no change to the shared component.

### Every refetch unmounts the whole table and replaces it with the word "Loading…", the exact regression the shared DataTable was changed to prevent. — `(platform)/super-admin/users/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `missing-state` · line 318
- **What a user sees:** Typing collapses the table to one line and the page jumps, on every search, status and role change; same two lines at subscriptions/page.tsx:368-369 and attendance/page.tsx:420-421.
- **Evidence:** page.tsx:317-318 `) : loading ? (` / `<p className="text-sm text-muted">Loading…</p>`; `useCollection` sets `setLoading(true)` at the top of its effect; the debounce fires 300 ms after typing stops (:135-141).
- **Fix:** `<LoadingBlock />` only when `loading && rows.length === 0`, otherwise keep the table mounted with `busy={loading}`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** Lines 317-318 are verbatim, the debounce effect is exactly at 135-141, and useCollection.ts:151 (not the cited 150, which is blank) is `setLoading(true);` at the top of the query effect. table.tsx:12-15 is quoted exactly — "**A refresh does not blank the screen.** The old list screens replaced the whole table with the word \"Loading…\" on every debounced keystroke … `DataTable` now dims in place while `busy`, and the skeleton is only for the first load" — and the props really support it (`busy?: boolean` at :161, applied as `${busy ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-60' : ''}`). `busy={loading}` is passed by exactly one list screen, payments/page.tsx:418, which also uses `LoadingBlock` at :394; users, subscriptions and attendance all use the blanking ternary. The regression and the fix are real.

### No column is marked `primary`, so below 768px every card is headed by the attendance date instead of the student's name. — `(school)/school/attendance/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `mobile` · line 216
- **What a user sees:** On a phone with the date filter set — the screen's main workflow — every card is titled with the same bold date and the child's name is demoted to a dt/dd row.
- **Evidence:** table.tsx picks `columns.find((c) => c.primary) ?? columns[0]` and renders it as the card heading; attendance/page.tsx's first column is `attendance_date` (:216) and the `student` column (:229) sets no `primary`.
- **Fix:** Add `primary: true` to the `student` column; consider `hideOnMobile: true` on `marked_at`.

A grep of src/app for `primary: true` returns one hit only — payments/page.tsx:176 — so attendance sets none, and its columns array begins `key: 'attendance_date'` at 216 with `key: 'student'` at 229. Impact follows directly at Tailwind's md breakpoint (768px).

### The three hand-rolled selects and the textarea carry no aria-invalid or aria-describedby, so their server-side errors are announced nowhere and paint no error border. — `(school)/school/classes/new/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed**
- **Category:** `a11y` · line 344
- **What a user sees:** A screen-reader user tabbing to the select hears only "Academic session, combo box" with no error, and the invalid control is the one that looks fine while `name`/`code`/`capacity` on the same form do turn red.
- **Evidence:** classes/new/page.tsx:344-351 `<select id="academic_session_id" required disabled={sessions.state === 'loading'} value={values.academic_session_id} onChange={set('academic_session_id')} className={INPUT_CLASS}>` — and identically at 443, 497 and the textarea at 522. The error is an unassociated sibling at 370-373.
- **Fix:** Replace the three `<select>` blocks with `SelectField` and the `<textarea>` with `TextAreaField` from components/form.tsx.
- **Why it survived refutation:** All four quotes are verbatim; `grep -n INPUT_CLASS` on the file returns exactly 104 (the definition) and the four use sites 350, 448, 501, 528, and none of those blocks contains `aria-invalid`. INPUT_CLASS is `'mt-1 w-full rounded-md border px-3 py-2 outline-none transition-colors ' + 'field-input '`, so the styling hook is present and only the attribute is missing — globals.css:644-647 `.field-input[aria-invalid="true"], .field-select[aria-invalid="true"], .field-textarea[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: var(--danger); }` fires on nothing. The asymmetry claim holds: form.tsx sets `aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}` at 106 (Field), 150 (SelectField), 192 (TextAreaField) and 271, so the `Field`-based `name`/`code`/`capacity` controls on the same form do go red while the selects do not. SelectField's quoted block at 146-153 is verbatim.

(<p className="mt-1 text-sm text-danger">{fieldErrors.class_id}</p>` at 410-411, and no `aria-invalid` appears anywhere in the file. The same INPUT_CLASS pattern applies (`'... transition-colors ' + 'field-input '` at 114-116), so the CSS rule exists and the attribute that triggers it does not. ### The search box has no debounce, so every keystroke fires a request and blanks the table — every other search screen in this codebase debounces. — `(school)/school/documents/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 158
- **What a user sees:** Typing a ten-character title fires ten requests and the results table vanishes and reappears on every character, so the user cannot read partial matches as they type.
- **Evidence:** documents/page.tsx:74 `const [search, setSearch] = useState('');` feeds straight into the query at 77-80, and 158 writes it on every change: `onChange={(event) => { setSearch(event.target.value); setPage(1); }}`. The table is replaced wholesale at 184-185.
- **Fix:** Add the 300 ms `debounced` state the sibling screens use, and pass `busy` to `DataTable` instead of unmounting it.

There is no intermediate state — `grep -rn "const \[debounced" app/` lists 16 files and documents/page.tsx is not among them, while every sibling search screen is (exams:155, students:157, teachers:145, parents:104, staff:131, homework:148, timetable:120, plus eight platform screens). useCollection.ts refetches on every value change: lines 147-150 are verbatim `useEffect(() => {` / `const controller = new AbortController();` / blank / `setLoading(true);`, and it holds no timer of its own. The remedy the fix names is already available: components/table.tsx:161 declares `busy?: boolean;`, :164 destructures it, and :171 applies `${busy ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-60' : ''}` — its header comment at :14 says "`DataTable` now dims in place while `busy`". env.js:206 is `max: num('RATE_LIMIT_MAX', 1000)` inside a `rateLimit` block whose `windowMinutes` default is 15, so the rate figure is right too.

### Internal navigation uses raw anchors instead of next/link, forcing a full document reload that discards the in-memory access token on every click. — `(school)/school/classes/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `ux` · line 253
- **What a user sees:** Clicking "Add class", "Cancel" or "Add section" tears down the whole SPA: a white flash, a full JS re-parse, then a refresh-token round-trip plus `GET /auth/me` before the form can fetch its pickers.
- **Evidence:** classes/page.tsx:252-257 `<a href="/school/classes/new" className="btn btn-primary">Add class</a>`; same at classes/new/page.tsx:551, exams/new/page.tsx:587 and sections/page.tsx:155. lib/apiClient.ts:153 `let accessToken: string | null = null;`
- **Fix:** Import `Link from 'next/link'` and replace these four anchors, keeping the same `className`.
- **Why it survived refutation:** All four anchors are verbatim (classes/page.tsx:253, classes/new:551 `<a href="/school/classes" className="text-sm underline underline-offset-2">`, exams/new:587, sections:155). apiClient.ts:153 is verbatim `let accessToken: string | null = null;` under the comment "In memory, never in `localStorage`", and the reload cost is real — :210-214 records that the backend "rotates the refresh token on every use and **treats reuse of an old one as theft**". next/link is genuinely the codebase's model where it matters: `grep -rln "from 'next/link'"` returns components/shell.tsx, app/(school)/school/page.tsx, app/(teacher)/teacher/page.tsx and the not-found pages. The pattern is widespread (49 raw internal anchors app-wide), but unlike an unbuilt feature this is behaviour that is wrong on every click, and the fix is a one-line import with no design change.

### A local money() helper duplicates lib/money.ts and drops thousands grouping, so large amounts render as an unreadable digit run. — `(school)/school/fees/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)
- **Category:** `money` · line 92
- **What a user sees:** 1250000 renders as 1250000.00 rather than 1,250,000.00; in a column of such figures an order-of-magnitude misread is easy.
- **Evidence:** fees/page.tsx:92-100 is verbatim, including `{value.toFixed(2)} <span className="text-muted-soft">{currency}</span>`, used at :128, :201, :202, :203 and :271. lib/money.ts:37-40 builds `const DECIMAL = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });` once, and :57-64 exports formatAmountWithCode; the file header at :1-2 says it exists "because three screens each had their own and two were wrong". money() is DECIMAL(14,2) — models/columns.js:60-61 `const money = (options = {}) => ({ type: DataTypes.DECIMAL(14, 2),` — so twelve digits before the point are possible.
- **Fix:** Delete the local helper and use formatAmountWithCode from @/lib/money.
- **Why it survived refutation:** lib/money.ts:1 reads "Money formatting, in one place, because three screens each had their own and two were wrong." and the shared formatter pins locale 'en-US', so it carries no hydration risk the local helper avoids. Citation drift of one line only (formatter at 37-40, not 38-41); the DECIMAL(14,2) definition lives at models/columns.js:60-61, not at models/finance.js:132-138 as cited — that range is where finance.js *calls* money().

### The same local money() duplicate here drops thousands grouping from the ledgers and from the three Net Balance cards. — `(school)/school/finance/page.tsx`

- **Status:** **Closed** in session 28 — fixed; reviewed against the backend it calls, type-checked and linted; not reproduced in a browser (signing in needs a password this session does not enter)

- **What a user sees:** The headline Net Balance prints as 4835000.00 with no separators — the hardest form to read and the easiest to misjudge by a factor of ten.

Used at :143 (Amount column) and :295, :299, :308 (Income / Expense / Net balance). lib/money.ts:50 exports formatMoney and :57 formatAmountWithCode, both grouped through the Intl formatter at :37-40. finance/page.tsx:288 is `  const currency = report.currency ?? '';`, so a null report currency renders the figure followed by a space and an empty span.
